Let's implement L-BFGS on `u` length n-1 reference with epsilon.

Loss:
```
def _softmax_ref(u,n,eps):
    # u length n-1, y_n=0
    z = u.copy(); z=np.maximum(z, -700); z=np.minimum(z, 700)
    # subtract max for stability including 0 reference
    m=max(z.max(),0)
    exps=np.exp(z-m)
    exps_full=np.concatenate((exps, np.array([np.exp(-m)]))) # since ref 0 -> exp(-m)
    Z=sum(exp)
    p=(1-eps)*exp_full/Z + eps/n
    # ensure sum 1
    p/=p.sum()
    return p
```

LP expansion algorithm:
```
def lp_expand(p, M, n):
   # p normalized, add eps to ensure >0
   f = p
   M = max_conv(f) * (1+1e-12)
   Build A: for k 0..L-1, A[k,i]=f_{k-i} if 0<=k-i<n else 0.
   Solve linprog maximize -sum b? scipy minimizes, so c=-ones; A_ub=A, b_ub=M; bounds b≥0.
   If success and sum_b<1e9: b=...
   b/=sum_b
   return b, sum_b
```

Grad:

def _lbfgs_grad(x,n,ks,eps,lam):
    p,s = _softmax_p...
    c = _conv...
    h = ks*(c-cmax)/cmax
    exph = np.exp(h)
    Z=exph.sum()
    w=exph/Z
    v = _grad_p_from_weights(p,w,n) # cross corr
    g_p = 2.0 * v
    dot_g_s = np.dot(g_p, s) # since derivative uses s, not p? Check ∂G/∂y = (1-eps) s_i(g_i - dot(g,s)). If g=∂G/∂p.
    grad = (1-eps) * s * (g_p - dot_g_s) + lam * x
    return grad
```

from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix, csr_matrix, diags? 
def lp_direction(p, n):
    L=2*n-1
    f = p + 1e-12
    fsum=f.sum(); f/=fsum
    c = conv(f,n); M=c.max()
    # if M<=0 return
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    # For each variable i, nonzeros k=i..i+n-1 coefficient f_{k-i}
    # Use arange views: For i, k=i+j, j=0..n-1, coeff=f[j]
    # data = tile f? We can create 2D? Use np.repeat.
    cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
    rows = np.tile(np.arange(n), n) + np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)? Wait rows for variable i: i, i+1,... i+n-1. We can generate:
    rows = (np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)[:,None] + np.tile(np.arange(n), n)[None,:]).ravel()
    data = np.tile(f, n) # since coefficients f_j repeated for each variable.
    A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(L,n))
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)  # minimize -sum
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M), method='highs', bounds=(0,None))
```

   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(p)-1)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   p_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)

Let's implement `lp_improve(p, n, time_alloc)`:
```
def lp_improve(p, n, time_alloc):
    p = normalize(clip)
    best=p; best_score=exact_score_norm(p,n)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_alloc:
        M = max_conv(p)
        b, ok, sum_b = lp_direction(p,n)
        if not ok: break
        bnorm=b/sum_b
        # line search over mixes
        improved=False
        for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
            q=(1-t)*p + t*bnorm
            q=normalize
            s=exact_score(q,n)
            if s < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=s; best=q; improved=True; break? choose best among t maybe
        if not improved:
            # maybe try smaller t adapt
            # if no improve, break or reduce tolerance? 
            break
        p=best.copy()
        it+=1
        # optional: if sum_b not much >1? break
    return best,best_score
```

def active_gradient_polish(p,n,time_alloc, tol_frac=0.05, eta0=0.1):
    best=p; best_score=exact_score
    while time:
        c=conv; cmax=c.max()
        # weights active: threshold cmax*(1-tol_frac) maybe plus small
        mask = c >= cmax*(1-tol_frac)
        w = np.zeros_like(c); w[mask]=1; w/=w.sum()
        # simple smoothing: weights = softmax((c-cmax)/tau) with tau=cmax*tol_frac? 
        v=cross_corr(p,w,n); grad=2*v; grad-=grad.mean()
        # multiplicative update: p *= exp(-eta*grad); normalize
        # line search: try eta in [0.5*eta,...]
```

Convolution function:
```
def _linear_conv_fft(a, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    size=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size, n=L) # Does irfft accept n? yes.
    if c.size != L: c = c[:L]
    c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    return c
```
`irfft(..., n=L)` may output length L but if L not power, returns L. Good. But using `n=L` when size >L? irfft with n smaller than size truncates? It should. For safety, `c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)[:L]`.

Define:
```
def _lbfgs_loss_grad(x, n, ks, eps, r, nonref):
    p,s = _p_from_params(...)
    c = _linear_conv_fft(p,n)
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <= 0: cmax=1e-300
    h = ks * (c - cmax) / cmax
    h = np.clip(h, -745, 0)
    e = np.exp(h)
    Z = e.sum()
    # If Z too small? at least 1 because max h=0 (cmax). If multiple cmax, ok.
    logZ = np.log(Z)
    loss = cmax + (cmax/ks)*(logZ - np.log(2*n-1))
    # weights normalized? For grad, w=e/Z
    w = e / Z   # sum1
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, size) # we had A in conv; need return from conv or recompute.
```

def _convolution_fft(p,n, A=None):
    L=2*n-1; size=...
    if A is None: A=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=irfft(A*A,size)[:L]
    c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    return c,A
```

for j,idx in enumerate(nonref):
    grad[j] = one_minus_eps * s[idx] * (g[idx] - dot)
# If grad contains NaN? set zeros.
return float(loss), grad
```

Active gradient function can be improved with exact line search:
```
def active_subgradient(p,n,time_alloc,tau=0.1,eta=1.0):
    best=p; best_score=exact
    t0=time.monotonic(); no=0
    while time-...:
        c,_ = conv(p)
        cmax=c.max()
        # weights: if tau<=0, top active uniform; else softmax ((c-cmax)/(cmax*tau))
        if tau >0: h=(c-cmax)/(cmax*tau); w=exp(clip(h,-745,0)); w/=sum
        else: mask=c>=cmax - 1e-8; w=...
        v = cross_corr(p,w); grad=2*v - mean
        # multiplicative line search
        improved=False
        factors=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
        for factor in factors:
            q=p * np.exp(-eta*factor*(grad-mean? wait grad already centered? Multiplicative centering: sg_centered = grad - grad.mean(); q=p*exp(-eta*sg_centered); normalize.
            s=exact(q)
            if s<best_score-1e-12: best; improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            eta*=0.7; if eta<0.01: change tau; if many no reduce tau
```
This is the previous but with active weights. It can polish. Use after L-BFGS.

Let's think about shape and possible exact target. Could there be a simpler closed form near 1.5? Suppose we choose p as derivative of a flat polynomial square? For discrete, maybe choose p_i ∝ 1 - cos(2πi/n)/? Let's test via DFT: To keep c flat, we want A_m^2 small for most m. If p_i is a low-pass in time? Maybe p_i = delta? Not. Perhaps p_i ∝ (1 - exp(-2π i m0 (2i/n-1)))? Hmm.

Maybe use a sequence from "least variance" of cumulative sums. Let S_i = ∑_{j≤i} p_j. c_k = ∑ p_i p_{k-i}. Another view: c_k is local quadratic. To make flat, maybe p_i = S_i - S_{i-1} where S is approximate CDF with second derivative? If p_i is derivative of a function whose product? There are "maximum entropy" distributions with flat pair sums: Choose pairs i+j = k each have probability 1/(2n). This is a contingency table of nonnegative rank-1 matrix M_{i,j}=p_i p_j; we need sums over diagonals i+j=k constant. This is like approximating a doubly diagonal-stochastic matrix by rank-1 tensor. The matrix of pair outcomes is rank 1 nonnegative. We want all diagonals equal. This is exactly finding a nonnegative vector p such that its outer product has equal diagonal sums. The full matrix is n×n with p_i p_j. Diagonal sums c_k. Continuous analog: find f such that integral over hyperbolas f(x)f(y) constant. This is a "multiplicative" solution maybe f(t) ∝ 1/√(t(1-t))? Implement `subgradient_polish`:
```
def subgradient_polish(p,n,time_alloc, verbose=False):
    best=p; best_score=exact_neg_inf? 
    t0=time.monotonic(); eta=max(1.0,n/10.0); ks_sg=20; no=0
    while time < time_alloc:
       c,_=conv; cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<=0: break
       h=ks_sg*(c-cmax)/cmax; h=clip(h,-700,0); w=exp(h); w/=sum
       v=cross(p,w); grad=2*v; grad -= grad.mean()
       improved=False
       for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
           step=eta*mult
           q=p*np.exp(-step*grad)
           qsum=q.sum(); if qsum<: continue
           q/=qsum
           s=exact(q)
           if s<best_score -1e-12:
                best_score=s; best=q; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           p=best.copy(); no=0; eta=min(eta*1.05, 2*n) # small increase
       else:
           no+=1
           eta*=0.7
           if no%5==0: ks_sg=ks_sg*0.5 or change tol
           if no>50 or eta<1: break
       if verbose: print(...)
    return best,best_score
```
But `grad` centered; if grad has large max due finite precision, step*grad may overflow? grad ~1/n ~0.0005, eta~200 =>0.1. fine. If eta grows, cap n maybe.

Helper `clean_p(p)`:
```
def clean_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-12:
       p=np.ones_like(p); s=p.sum()
    p/=s
    return p
```
Use `n` known? If size mismatch? We can pass n.

`get_parent_sequence()`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       return None
    arr=arr.reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    if arr.size<2: return None
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    return arr/s
```

`resample_sequence(p, n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(p,n):
    p=clean_p(p)
    if p.size==n: m=p.copy()
    else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       m=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
    m=np.maximum(m,0.0)
    s=m.sum(); if s<...; m/=s
    return m
```

`_fft_size(n)`:
```
def _fft_size(n):
    L=2*n-1
    return 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
```
For n=1? not used.

`_convolution_fft(a, n)`:
```
def _convolution_fft(a,n):
    size=_fft_size(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:2*n-1]
    c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    return c,A
```
`_weighted_gradient(p,w,n,A=None)`:
```
def _weighted_gradient(p,w,n,A=None):
    size=_fft_size(n)
    if A is None: A=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    v=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,size)[:n]
    return v
```
Check A*r? For real p, A could have tiny imag. yes.

`_exact_score(p,n)`:
```
def _exact_score(p,n):
    c,_=_convolution_fft(p,n)
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
```
If max negative due p zero, return large. For p sum1, max positive.

`_params_from_p(p,n,eps,r)`:
```
def _params_from_p(p,n,eps,r):
    e=eps/n
    pr=p[r]-e
    if pr<=1e-300: pr=1e-300
    nonref=np.arange(n); nonref=nonref[nonref!=r]
    vals=p[nonref]-e
    vals=np.maximum(vals,1e-300)
    u=np.log(vals/pr)
    u=np.clip(u, -700.0, 700.0)
    return u, nonref
```

`_p_from_params(u,n,eps,r,nonref)`:
```
def _p_from_params(u,n,eps,r,nonref):
    m=0.0
    if u.size:
       um=max(m, float(np.max(u)))
    else: um=m
    exp_non=np.exp(np.clip(u-um, -745.0, 0.0))
    s=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
    s[nonref]=exp_non
    s[r]=np.exp(-um)
    Z=s.sum()
    if Z<=0: s=np.full(n,1.0/n)
    else: s/=Z
    p=(1.0-eps)*s + eps/n
    # ensure sum exactly? psum = p.sum(); p /= psum maybe slight mismatch. Since s sum1 and eps sum eps, p sum = (1-eps)+eps=1 exactly modulo. If s normalized with division, exact. No renorm? Numeric psum may dev 1e-15; gradient formula uses s. If we renorm p, mismatch small. We can renorm p for conv but not for gradient? Loss/grad both use same p, renorm could affect loss but not grad if not done in both. In `_lbfgs_loss_grad`, we compute p,s; if we renorm p, pass same p to conv and gradient? Gradient formula derived for p without renorm. But p sum difference tiny. We can skip renorm.
    return p,s
```
Could `s[r]` if um=0, s[r]=1; okay.

`_lbfgs_loss_grad(x,n,ks,eps,r,nonref)`:
Need if x has NaN/inf. If not finite, return 1e100, zeros.
```
def _lbfgs_loss_grad(x,n,ks,eps,r,nonref):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return (1e100, np.zeros(len(nonref)))
    p,s=_p_from_params(...)
    # if not finite p, fallback
    c,A=_convolution_fft(p,n)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0:
        return (1e100, zeros)
    h=ks*(c-cmax)/cmax
    h=np.clip(h, -745.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(h)
    Z=float(e.sum())
    if Z<=0 or not finite: e[np.argmax(c)]=1; Z=1
    L=2*n-1
    loss=cmax + (cmax/ks)*(np.log(Z)-np.log(L))
    w=e/Z
    v=_weighted_gradient(p,w,n,A)
    g=2.0*v
    # due numerical, g may be tiny negative where p? not issue.
    dot=float(np.dot(g,s))
    grad=np.empty(len(nonref))
    om_e=1-eps
    for j,idx in enumerate(nonref):
        grad[j]=om_e*s[idx]*(g[idx]-dot)
    # if nonfinite, zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       grad=np.zeros_like(grad)
    return float(loss), grad
```
Potential optimize loop over nonref in Python O(n) per eval; 10000*2048=20M okay. Could vectorize:
```
grad = om_e * s[nonref] * (g[nonref]-dot)
```
Yes. Use vectorized. Good.
`np.log(L)` each call; okay. Could precompute in args? Add `logL`? Not necessary. Could pass `logL` to avoid. But okay.

`lbfgs_anneal(p0,n,time_alloc)`:
```
def lbfgs_anneal(p,n,time_alloc):
    p=clean_p(p)
    best=p; best_score=_exact_score(p,n)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if n <= 256:
       stages=[(20,3000),(60,3000),(200,3000),(800,4000),(3000,4000)]
    elif n <= 1024:
       stages=[(20,2500),(60,2500),(200,2500),(800,3000),(3000,3000)]
    else:
       stages=[(20,2000),(60,2000),(200,2000),(800,2500),(3000,2500)]
    # If time_alloc small, reduce maxfun proportionally? base.
    scale = max(0.1, min(1.0, time_alloc/300.0)) # if alloc 100s scale 0.33
    for ks, base MF in stages:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_alloc*0.9: break
       # choose r = argmax(p) or if p[r] small? argmax
       r=int(np.argmax(p))
       # To avoid same r if max tied? ok.
       u,nonref=_params_from_p(p,n,EPS_OPT,r)
       MF=int(max(50, base_MF*max(0.2, min(1.0, time_alloc/600.0)))) # maybe
       try:
          res=scipy.optimize.minimize(_lbfgs_loss_grad, u, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(n,ks,EPS_OPT,r,nonref), options={'maxiter': MF, 'maxfun': MF, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':30})
       except Exception as e:
          print('L-BFGS error', e); break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          pn,_=_p_from_params(res.x,n,EPS_OPT,r,nonref)
          pn=clean_p(pn)
          sn=_exact_score(pn,n)
          p=pn
          if sn < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=sn; best=pn.copy()
       # print every stage? to avoid spam, maybe print stage score.
    return best,best_score
```

`subgradient_polish(p,n,time_alloc)`: as above. Need exact each candidate. To avoid using too much time, limit iterations by time.
```
def subgradient_polish(p,n,time_alloc):
    p=clean_p(p); best=p; best_score=_exact_score(p,n)
    t0=time.monotonic(); eta=max(1.0, n/5.0)
    ks=20.0; no=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_alloc:
       if time_alloc remaining <0.5: break
       c,A=_convolution_fft(p,n); cmax=...
       h=ks*(c-cmax)/cmax; h=clip; w=...;
       v=_weighted_gradient(p,w,n,A); grad=2*v; grad-=mean
       improved=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
          step=eta*mult
          # if step*max|grad| > 5, skip? maybe
          q=p*np.exp(-step*grad)
          q=np.maximum(q,0); qs=q.sum(); if qs<=0 continue; q/=qs
          s=_exact_score(q,n)
          if s < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=s; best=q; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          p=best.copy(); no=0
          # small increase
          eta=min(eta*1.1, 10.0*n)
          # occasionally vary ks
          if len(best history?) not.
       else:
          no +=1
          eta *= 0.7
          if no % 7 ==0:
             ks = max(1.0, ks*0.5)
             if ks < 3: ks=100.0  # cycle?
          if no > 80 or eta < 1.0:
             break
    # final threshold tiny
    p=best.copy(); p[np.abs(p)<1e-15]=0; s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/=s
    return p,_exact_score(p,n)
```

`lp_improve(p,n,time_alloc)`:
```
def lp_improve(p,n,time_alloc):
    p=clean_p; best=p; best_score=exact
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time <- time_alloc and it<200:
       f=(1-1e-8)*p + 1e-8/n; f/=f.sum()
       c,_=_convolution_fft(f,n); M=float(np.max(c)); if M<=0: break
       L=2*n-1
       A=np.zeros((L,n))
       # mask? For i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i]=f
       for i in range(n):
           A[i:i+n,i]=f
       # Solve max b' s.t. A b' <=1, b'>=0
       try:
          res=scipy.optimize.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(L), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       except Exception: break
       if not res.success: break
       b=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       b=np.where(np.isfinite(b)&(b>0),b,0)
       s=b.sum()
       if not finite or s<=1+1e-6: break
       g=b/s
       # line search
       old_score=best_score
       improved=False
       best_q=p; best_s=old_score
       for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0):
           q=(1-t)*best_q? Actually base should p (current best) not updated within mixes? Use p_best.
           q=(1-t)*best + t*g
           q=clean_p(q)
           sq=exact(q)
           if sq<best_s:
               best_s=sq; best_q=q
       if best_s < old_score -1e-12:
           best=best_q.copy(); best_score=best_s; improved=True
       if not improved: break
       it+=1
       # optional if s not increasing? no tracking
    return best,best_score
```
Note in line search, base should be current best p, not updated within t. We set `base=best` before mixes. Good.

Function `run_length(n, alloc)`:
```
def optimize_length(n, alloc, parent, global_best, rng):
    start_candidates=[]
    if global_best is not None: start_candidates.append(resample(global_best,n))
    if parent is not None:
       pr=resample(parent,n)
       if not start_candidates or not close: append(pr)

`optimize_one_start(p,n,alloc,strict=True)`:

def optimize_one_start(p,n,alloc):
    p=clean_p(p); best=p; best_s=_exact_score(p,n)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # If alloc<15, just return
    # L-BFGS
    lf_time = min(alloc*0.75, alloc-20) if alloc>40 else alloc*0.9
    p1,s1=lbfgs_anneal(p,n,lf_time)
    if s1<best_s: best,best_s=p1,s1
    rem = alloc - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if n<=512 and rem>30:
       lp_time=min(rem*0.3, 120)
       p2,s2=lp_improve(best,n,lp_time)
       ...
    # subgrad remaining
    rem=alloc - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem>10:
       p3,s3=subgradient_polish(best,n,rem)
       ...
    # Final exact maybe threshold
    p3[np.abs(p3)<1e-15]=0; normalize
    s3=_exact_score(p3,n)
    if s3<best_s: best=p3; best_s=s3
    return best,best_s
```

`add_candidate` helper top-level:
```
def add_candidate(lst, p, tol=0.05):
   p=clean_p(p)
   if p.size==0: return lst
   for q in lst:
      diff=np.linalg.norm(p-q)
      if diff < tol: return lst
   lst.append(p)
   return lst
```

Need top-level normalize:
```
def _normalize_template(raw):
    raw=np.asarray(raw,dtype=float)
    raw=np.where(np.isfinite(raw),raw,0.0)
    raw=np.maximum(raw,0.0)
    s=raw.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    raw/=s
    return raw
```
Use in templates.

In `make_templates`, no nested.

for alpha in [1.0,0.5,2.0]: raw=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n)*alpha)

`collect_starts`:
```
def collect_starts(n, global_best, parent, rng, max_count):
    chosen=[]
    if global_best is not None: add_candidate(chosen, resample_sequence(global_best,n))
    if parent is not None: add_candidate(chosen, resample_sequence(parent,n))
    if chosen:
        base=chosen[0]
        noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.15,base.size)
        raw=np.exp(np.clip(noise,-4,4))*base # or base*(1+noise)
        add_candidate(chosen, base*(1+noise))
    templates=make_templates(n,rng,max_count=max(1,max_count+2))
    scored=[]
    for tp in templates:
       s=_exact_score(tp,n)
       scored.append((s,tp))
    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    for s,tp in scored:
       add_candidate(chosen,tp, tol=0.08)
       if len(chosen)>=max_count: break
    if not chosen: add uniform
    return chosen[:max_count]
```
Tol maybe 0.1 for diversity. Since probabilities length large, l2 norm difference between different templates ~0.1-0.3. Use 0.1. For parent and perturbed may distance 0.15. okay.

`add_candidate` top-level:
```
def add_candidate(lst,p,tol=0.1):
   p=clean_p(p)
   if p.size==0: return lst
   for q in lst:
      if q.size==p.size and np.linalg.norm(p-q) < tol:
          return lst
   lst.append(p)
   return lst
```

Now, `consider_update`:
```
def consider_update(p, best_p, best_score):
    if p is None: return best_p,best_score
    p=clean_p(p)
    if p.size==0: return best_p,best_score
    s=_exact_score(p,len(p))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score - 1e-12:
        return p.copy(), s
    return best_p,best_score
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
  2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The code uses several complementary local optimizers:
  1. L-BFGS on a smoothed max-convolution surrogate, with fixed-reference
     softmax parameters to respect nonnegativity and sum-to-1 normalization.
  2. Active/subgradient multiplicative polishing with backtracking line search
     on the exact objective.
  3. A linear-programming expansion step for moderate n, inspired by the
     previous SOTA LP damping idea.
  4. Multiple sequence lengths and diverse starts (parent, resamples,
     skewed templates, random Dirichlet, edge-enhanced profiles).

The exact objective is scale invariant, so all internal searches keep the
sequence normalized to sum 1. The final returned vector is nonnegative and
safe for evaluate_sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize
from operator import itemgetter

EPS_OPT = 1e-7          # small positivity floor used in the L-BFGS parameterization
MAX_FINAL_N = 4096      # hard cap for the final dyadic refinement


def _clean_p(p):
    """Clip, make finite, normalize to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.sum())
    p /= s
    return p


def _get_parent_sequence():
    """Return normalized parent sequence height_sequence_1 if available."""
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _fft_size(n):
    """Next power of two at least 2n-1."""
    L = 2 * n - 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def _convolution_fft(a, n):
    """Linear convolution a*a using FFT. Returns (conv, fft_a)."""
    size = _fft_size(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.real(c[:L])
    return c, A


def _exact_score(p, n):
    """Internal exact objective for sum(p)=1."""
    c, _ = _convolution_fft(p, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * cmax


def _cross_gradient_weights(p, w, n, A=None):
    """
    Given probability weights w over convolution lags, return v_i such that
    v_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}. This is the pre-gradient factor for
    d/dp of sum_k w_k c_k, whose full gradient with respect to p is 2*v.
    """
    size = _fft_size(n)
    if A is None:
        A = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    v = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, size)
    return np.real(v[:n])


def _params_from_p(p, n, eps, r):
    """
    Convert probability p to fixed-reference log-parameters.
    Coordinate r is fixed to log 0; other coordinates are u_i = log(p_i/p_r)
    with a small epsilon floor.
    """
    e = eps / float(n)
    pr = float(p[r]) - e
    if pr <= 1e-300:
        pr = 1e-300

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    nonref = idx[idx != r]
    vals = p[nonref] - e
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-300)
    u = np.log(vals / pr)
    u = np.clip(u, -700.0, 700.0)
    return u, nonref


def _p_from_params(u, n, eps, r, nonref):
    """
    Invert fixed-reference log-parameters:
      p_i = (1-eps) s_i + eps/n, with s a softmax and s_r = exp(0)/Z.
    """
    if u.size == 0:
        s = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        m = float(np.max(u))
        if m < 0.0:
            m = 0.0
        exp_non = np.exp(np.clip(u - m, -745.0, 0.0))
        s = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s[nonref] = exp_non
        s[r] = np.exp(-m)
        Z = float(s.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            s = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            s /= Z

    p = (1.0 - eps) * s + eps / float(n)
    return p, s


def _lbfgs_loss(x, n, ks, eps, r, nonref):
    """Smoothed convex-combination surrogate of max convolution."""
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    p, _ = _p_from_params(x, n, eps, r, nonref)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return 1e100

    c, _ = _convolution_fft(p, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e100

    h = float(ks) * (c - cmax) / cmax
    h = np.clip(h, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(h)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
        Z = 1.0

    L = 2 * n - 1
    loss = cmax + (cmax / float(ks)) * (np.log(Z) - np.log(float(L)))
    if not np.isfinite(loss):
        return 1e100
    return float(loss)


def _lbfgs_grad(x, n, ks, eps, r, nonref):
    """Gradient of the smoothed surrogate with respect to fixed-reference u."""
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(len(nonref), dtype=np.float64)

    p, s = _p_from_params(x, n, eps, r, nonref)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return np.zeros(len(nonref), dtype=np.float64)

    c, A = _convolution_fft(p, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(len(nonref), dtype=np.float64)

    h = float(ks) * (c - cmax) / cmax
    h = np.clip(h, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(h)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / Z

    v = _cross_gradient_weights(p, w, n, A=A)
    g = 2.0 * v
    dot = float(np.dot(g, s))
    grad_full = (1.0 - eps) * (2.0 * v * s[nonref] - s[nonref] * dot)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_full)):
        return np.zeros(len(nonref), dtype=np.float64)
    return grad_full


def _lbfgs_anneal(p0, n, time_alloc):
    """Run several L-BFGS stages with increasing smoothing sharpness."""
    p = _clean_p(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(p, n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if time_alloc <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    if n <= 256:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 2200),
            (60.0, 2200),
            (200.0, 2200),
            (800.0, 2600),
            (3000.0, 2600),
        ]
    elif n <= 1024:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 1800),
            (60.0, 1800),
            (200.0, 1800),
            (800.0, 2200),
            (3000.0, 2200),
        ]
    else:
        stages = [
            (20.0, 1400),
            (60.0, 1400),
            (200.0, 1500),
            (800.0, 1800),
            (3000.0, 1800),
        ]

    mf_scale = max(0.15, min(2.0, time_alloc / 250.0))

    for ks, base_mf in stages:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_alloc * 0.92):
            break

        # Pick a reference coordinate with large mass to keep logs moderate.
        r = int(np.argmax(p))
        u, nonref = _params_from_p(p, n, EPS_OPT, r)
        mf = int(max(120, base_mf * mf_scale))

        try:
            res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
                _lbfgs_loss,
                u,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                args=(n, ks, EPS_OPT, r, nonref),
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(50, mf),
                    "maxfun": max(120, mf),
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("L-BFGS error:", exc)
            break

        if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            pn, _ = _p_from_params(res.x, n, EPS_OPT, r, nonref)
            pn = _clean_p(pn)
            sn = _exact_score(pn, n)

            if np.isfinite(sn):
                # Keep a safe work point: allow modest temporary worsening,
                # but do not follow catastrophically bad smoothed minima.
                if sn <= best_score + 0.45:
                    p = pn
                else:
                    p = best

                if sn < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sn
                    best = pn.copy()
                    print(f"    L-BFGS improve n={n} ks={ks:g} score={sn:.7f}")

    return best, best_score


def _subgradient_polish(p0, n, time_alloc):
    """
    Exact-subgradient / soft-peak multiplicative polish.
    Useful for sharpening the non-smooth max objective after L-BFGS.
    """
    p = _clean_p(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(p, n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_alloc <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    ks_values = [30.0, 80.0, 15.0, 200.0, 8.0, 500.0, 20.0, 1000.0]
    ks_idx = 0
    eta = max(1.0, float(n) / 8.0)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_alloc:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_alloc - 1.0):
            break

        c, A = _convolution_fft(p, n)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        ks = ks_values[ks_idx]
        h = ks * (c - cmax) / cmax
        h = np.clip(h, -745.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(h)
        Z = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / Z

        v = _cross_gradient_weights(p, w, n, A=A)
        grad = 2.0 * v
        grad -= grad.mean()

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015):
            step = eta * mult
            ex = -step * grad
            ex = np.clip(ex, -50.0, 50.0)
            q = p * np.exp(ex)
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if qs <= 1e-15:
                continue
            q /= qs
            qs_score = _exact_score(q, n)
            if qs_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = qs_score
                best = q.copy()
                p = q.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(eta * 1.08, 10.0 * float(n))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta *= 0.72
            if no_improve % 5 == 0:
                ks_idx = (ks_idx + 1) % len(ks_values)
                if eta < 1.0:
                    eta = max(1.0, float(n) / 8.0)
            if no_improve > 120 or eta < 0.15:
                break

    p = best.copy()
    p[np.abs(p) < 1e-15] = 0.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n) / float(n)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p, _exact_score(p, n)


def _lp_improve(p0, n, time_alloc):
    """
    LP expansion step for moderate n:
      maximize sum(b') s.t. f*b' <= 1, b' >= 0,
    where f is the current probability. The returned b' is normalized and
    mixed with the current point by a line search.
    """
    p = _clean_p(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(p, n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_alloc <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    L = 2 * n - 1

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max(0.0, time_alloc - 20.0):
        floor = 1e-8
        f = (1.0 - floor) * p + floor / float(n)
        f /= f.sum()

        c, _ = _convolution_fft(f, n)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = f

        try:
            res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "output_flag": False},
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            #print("LP error:", exc)
            break

        if not res.success or res.x is None:
            break

        b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b) & (b > 0.0), b, 0.0)
        bsum = float(b.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(bsum) or bsum <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
            break

        g = b / bsum

        base = best.copy()
        best_q = base.copy()
        best_s = best_score

        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
            q = (1.0 - t) * base + t * g
            q = _clean_p(q)
            sq = _exact_score(q, n)
            if sq < best_s:
                best_s = sq
                best_q = q.copy()

        if best_s < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = best_s
            best = best_q.copy()
        else:
            break

    return best, best_score


def _optimize_one_start(p0, n, time_alloc):
    """Run a complete mini-campaign from one initial sequence."""
    p = _clean_p(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(p, n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_alloc <= 1.0:
        return best, best_score

    # Main smooth L-BFGS anneal.
    lf_alloc = time_alloc * 0.75
    p1, s1 = _lbfgs_anneal(p, n, lf_alloc)
    if s1 < best_score:
        best, best_score = p1.copy(), s1

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = max(0.0, time_alloc - elapsed)

    # Moderate-n LP polish.
    if n <= 512 and rem > 35.0:
        lp_alloc = min(rem * 0.45, 120.0)
        p2, s2 = _lp_improve(best, n, lp_alloc)
        if s2 < best_score:
            best, best_score = p2.copy(), s2
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        rem = max(0.0, time_alloc - elapsed)

    # Exact right-joint/subgradient polish for the final part of the budget.
    if rem > 5.0:
        sg_alloc = min(rem, 180.0)
        p3, s3 = _subgradient_polish(best, n, sg_alloc)
        if s3 < best_score:
            best, best_score = p3.copy(), s3

    # Very small final cleanup: remove numerical dust.
    best = best.copy()
    best[np.abs(best) < 1e-15] = 0.0
    s = float(best.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        best = np.ones(n) / float(n)
    else:
        best /= s
    fs = _exact_score(best, n)
    if fs < best_score:
        best_score = fs

    return best, best_score


def _resample_sequence(p, n):
    """Linearly resample a nonnegative probability vector to length n."""
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p.size == n:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _clean_p(q)


def _normalize_template(raw):
    raw = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
    s = float(raw.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return raw / s


def _make_templates(n, rng, max_count=12):
    """Generate diverse asymmetric nonnegative initial shapes."""
    out = []

    def add(raw):
        q = _normalize_template(raw)
        if q is not None:
            out.append(q)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Uniform with noise.
    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.2 * rng.standard_normal(n))

    # Edge-enhanced profiles.
    for width in (0.07, 0.12, 0.20):
        for a in (1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
            raw = 0.25 + a * (np.exp(-t / width) + np.exp(-(1.0 - t) / width))
            add(raw)

    # Exponential tilts.
    for gamma in (-5.0, -3.0, -1.5, 1.5, 3.0, 5.0):
        raw = np.exp(np.clip(gamma * t, -60.0, 60.0))
        add(raw)

    # Skewed beta-like profiles.
    for aa, bb in [(0.5, 2.0), (2.0, 0.5), (0.3, 3.0), (3.0, 0.3),
                   (0.7, 1.6), (1.6, 0.7), (4.0, 1.0), (1.0, 4.0)]:
        raw = (t + 1e-3) ** aa * ((1.0 - t) + 1e-3) ** bb
        add(raw)

    # Shifted raised-cosine bumps. For a shifted phase these are asymmetric.
    for phi in (0.4, 1.1, 2.2, 2.9):
        for alpha in (1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
            base = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t + phi)
            base = np.maximum(base, 1e-12)
            raw = np.power(base, alpha) + 1e-4
            add(raw)

    # Two-mode profiles.
    for c1, s1, c2, s2, w1 in [
        (0.18, 0.07, 0.78, 0.18, 0.55),
        (0.30, 0.10, 0.72, 0.14, 0.40),
        (0.12, 0.05, 0.85, 0.25, 0.65),
        (0.55, 0.12, 0.20, 0.08, 0.45),
    ]:
        raw = (
            w1 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c1) / s1) ** 2)
            + (1.0 - w1) * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c2) / s2) ** 2)
            + 1e-4
        )
        add(raw)

    # Random Dirichlet starts.
    for alpha in (0.35, 0.75, 1.5):
        try:
            add(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * alpha))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not out:
        add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    return out[:max_count]


def _add_candidate(lst, p, tol=0.09):
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return lst
    for q in lst:
        if q.size == p.size and float(np.linalg.norm(p - q)) < tol:
            return lst
    lst.append(p)
    return lst


def _collect_starts(n, global_best, parent, rng, max_count):
    """Collect a diverse list of starting points for a given length."""
    chosen = []

    if global_best is not None:
        _add_candidate(chosen, _resample_sequence(global_best, n), tol=0.0)

    if parent is not None:
        _add_candidate(chosen, _resample_sequence(parent, n))

    # A perturbed version of the best available start.
    if chosen:
        base = chosen[0]
        noise = 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(base.size)
        raw = base * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -3.5, 3.5))
        _add_candidate(chosen, raw)

    # Templates, but keep the best-looking initial shapes first.
    templates = _make_templates(n, rng, max_count=max(6, max_count + 4))
    scored = []
    for tp in templates:
        sc = _exact_score(tp, n)
        scored.append((sc, tp))
    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    for sc, tp in scored:
        _add_candidate(chosen, tp)
        if len(chosen) >= max_count:
            break

    if not chosen:
        chosen.append(np.ones(n) / float(n))

    return chosen[:max_count]


def _consider_update(p, best_p, best_score):
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return best_p, best_score
    sc = _exact_score(p, len(p))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
        return p.copy(), sc
    return best_p, best_score


def _insert_pool(pool, p, sc, max_size=8):
    """Maintain a small pool of best internal candidates."""
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return pool
    for s0, q in pool:
        if q.size == p.size and float(np.linalg.norm(p - q)) < 1e-8:
            if sc < s0:
                pool[pool.index((s0, q))] = (sc, p)
            pool.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
            return pool[:max_size]
    pool.append((sc, p))
    pool.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    if len(pool) > max_size:
        pool.pop()
    return pool


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point. Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the
    provided evaluation function within the allotted wall time.
    """
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 60.0:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    # Leave a safety margin for final validation and Python overhead.
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget_s) - 35.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345 + int(seed) if seed is not None else 12345)

    parent = _get_parent_sequence()
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    pool = []

    # Initial fallback / seed candidates.
    for init_n in (128, 256):
        dummy = np.ones(init_n) / float(init_n)
        best_p, best_score = _consider_update(dummy, best_p, best_score)
        pool = _insert_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)

    if parent is not None:
        best_p, best_score = _consider_update(_clean_p(parent), best_p, best_score)
        pool = _insert_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)

    print(f"Initial bound: {best_score:.7f}")

    # Length schedule. Fractions are of the usable budget.
    schedule = [
        (128,  0.07, 3),
        (256,  0.11, 3),
        (512,  0.19, 4),
        (1024, 0.27, 4),
        (2048, 0.34, 4),
    ]

    usable_total = max(60.0, budget_s - 70.0)
    seen_lengths = set()

    for n, frac, starts in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80.0:
            break

        alloc = usable_total * frac
        alloc = min(alloc, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0))
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break

        # If this length was already represented as the current best, still
        # spend time on fresh starts to escape local minima.
        starts = max(2, min(starts, 5))
        candidates = _collect_starts(n, best_p, parent, rng, max_count=starts)
        seen_lengths.add(n)

        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 50.0)
        per_start = max(20.0, remaining / float(len(candidates)))

        print(f"Optimizing n={n} with {len(candidates)} starts, {alloc:.1f}s allocated")

        for idx, p0 in enumerate(candidates):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 50.0:
                break
            this_alloc = min(per_start, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 45.0))
            if this_alloc < 15.0:
                break

            p_out, s_out = _optimize_one_start(p0, n, this_alloc)
            old_score = best_score
            best_p, best_score = _consider_update(p_out, best_p, best_score)
            if best_score < old_score:
                pool = _insert_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)
                print(f"  n={n} start={idx}: improved to {best_score:.7f}")

            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break

    # Final refinement / dyadic enlargement of the best candidate.
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        p0 = _resample_sequence(best_p, len(best_p))
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0)
        p_out, s_out = _subgradient_polish(p0, len(best_p), min(rem, 90.0))
        best_p, best_score = _consider_update(p_out, best_p, best_score)
        pool = _insert_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)

    # If time remains and the sequence is not at the cap, try doubling length.
    while best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
        cur_n = len(best_p)
        if cur_n >= MAX_FINAL_N:
            break
        new_n = min(2 * cur_n, MAX_FINAL_N)
        p2 = _resample_sequence(best_p, new_n)
        # Add a tiny perturbation to avoid an identical low-resolution basin.
        perturb = 0.10 * rng.standard_normal(p2.size)
        p2 = _clean_p(p2 * np.exp(np.clip(perturb, -3.0, 3.0)))

        alloc = min(450.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 50.0))
        if alloc < 80.0:
            break

        old_score = best_score
        p_out, s_out = _optimize_one_start(p2, new_n, alloc)
        best_p, best_score = _consider_update(p_out, best_p, best_score)
        pool = _insert_pool(pool, best_p, best_score)
        print(f"Dyadic refine: {cur_n}->{new_n}, score={best_score:.7f}")

        if best_score >= old_score - 1e-8:
            # No improvement by doubling; stop enlarging.
            break

    # Final cleanup.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(256) / 256.0
    else:
        best_p = _clean_p(best_p)
        best_p[np.abs(best_p) < 1e-15] = 0.0
        best_p = np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(best_p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            best_p = np.ones(256) / 256.0
        else:
            best_p /= s

    internal_score = _exact_score(_clean_p(best_p), len(best_p))
    print(f"Internal final score: {internal_score:.7f} | n={len(best_p)}")

    # Validate with the provided evaluator on a small set of strong candidates.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_p is not None:
        final_candidates.append((internal_score, best_p.copy()))
    for sc, q in pool[:5]:
        if q is not None:
            final_candidates.append((sc, q))
    if parent is not None:
        final_candidates.append((1e100, _clean_p(parent)))
    final_candidates.append((1e100, np.ones(256) / 256.0))

    best_seq = None
    best_official = float("inf")

    unique = []
    for sc, seq in final_candidates:
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ok = True
        for s2, seq2 in unique:
            if seq.size == seq2.size and float(np.linalg.norm(seq - seq2)) < 1e-7:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok and seq.size > 0:
            unique.append((sc, seq))

    # Evaluate the best few candidates, but not too many huge ones.
    evaluated_large = 0
    unique.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    for i, (sc, seq) in enumerate(unique):
        if i >= 6:
            break
        if seq.size > 2048:
            if evaluated_large >= 2:
                continue
            evaluated_large += 1
        try:
            official = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
        except Exception:
            official = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(official) and official < best_official:
            best_official = official
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _clean_p(best_p)

    # Last sanity check.
    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = np.ones(256) / 256.0

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(best_seq.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0:
        best_seq = np.ones(256) / 256.0
    else:
        best_seq /= ss

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
